c) / (1.0 + np.exp(-a * (theta - b)))\\n\\n\\ndef estimate_theta_mle(responses, irt_params, item_indices, max_iter=100, tol=1e-6):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MLE of theta via Newton-Raphson on the 4PL model.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    theta = 0.0\\n    for _ in range(max_iter):\\n        grad = 0.0\\n        hess = 0.0\\n        for i, idx in enumerate(item_indices):\\n            p = irt_params[idx]\\n            a, b, c, d = p[\\\"a\\\"], p[\\\"b\\\"], p[\\\"c\\\"], p[\\\"d\\\"]\\n            P = irf_4pl(theta, a, b, c, d)\\n            P = np.clip(P, 1e-10, 1.0 - c) * sigma * (1.0 - sigma)\\n        h = dP / (P * Q)\\n        I_theta += P * Q * h ** 2\\n        C_theta += P * Q * h * g\\n    if Var_l0 < 1e-15:\\n        return 0.0\\n    Var_star = Var_l0 - C_theta ** 2 / I_theta if I_theta > 1e-15 else Var_l0\\n    if Var_star <= 0:\\n        Var_star = Var_l0\\n    return (l0 - load_irt_params(path):\n    params = {}\n    with open(path) as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            idx = int(row[\"item_idx\"])\n            params[idx] = {\n                \"a\": float(row[\"a_discrimination\"]),\n                \"b\": float(row[\"b_difficulty\"]),\n                \"c\": float(row[\"c_guessing\"]),\n                \"d\": float(row[\"d_feasibility\"]),\n            }\n    return params\n\ndef irf_4pl(theta, a, b, c, d):\n    return c + (d - c) / (1.0 + np.exp(-a * (theta - b)))\n\ndef compute_lz_drasgow(responses, irt_params, theta):\n    \"\"\"Drasgow's lz (uncorrected) — matches lz_fixed in theta_fixed_n10_consistent.json\"\"\"\n    l0 = 0.0\n    E_l0 = 0.0\n    Var_l0 = 0.0\n    for i in range(len(responses)):\n        if i not in irt_params:\n            continue\n        p = irt_params[i]\n        a, b, c, d = p[\"a\"], p[\"b\"], p[\"c\"], p[\"d\"]\n        P = irf_4pl(theta, a, b, c, d)\n        P = np.clip(P, 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)\n        Q = 1.0 - P\n        x = responses[i]\n        log_P = np.log(P)\n        log_Q = np.log(Q)\n        l0 += x * log_P + (1 - x) * log_Q\n compute_lz_star(responses, irt_params, theta):\n    \"\"\"lz* with Snijders (2001) variance correction\"\"\"\n    l0 = 0.0\n    E_l0 = 0.0\n    Var_l0 = 0.0\n    I_theta = 0.0\n    C_theta = 0.0\n    for i in range(len(responses)):\n        if i not in irt_params:\n            continue\n        p = irt_params[i]\n        a, b, c, d = p[\"a\"], p[\"b\"], p[\"c\"], p[\"d\"]\n        P = irf_4pl(theta, a, b, c, d)\n        P = np.clip(P, 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)\n        Q = 1.0 - P\n        x = responses[i]\n        log_P = np.log(P)\n        log_Q = np.log(Q)\n        l0 += x * log_P + (1 - x) * log_Q\n        E_l0 += P * log_P + Q * log_Q\n        g = log_P - log_Q\n        Var_l0 += P * Q * g ** 2\n        z = a * (theta - b)\n        sigma = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(z, -30, 30)))\n        dP = a * (d - c) * sigma * (1.0 - sigma)\n        h = dP / (P * Q)\n        I_theta += P * Q * h ** 2\n        C_theta += P * Q * h * g\n    if Var_l0 < 1e-15:\n        return 0.0\n    Var_star = Var_l0 - C_theta ** 2 / I_theta if I_theta > 1e-15 else Var_l0\n    if Var_star <= 0:\n        Var_star = Var_l0\n    return (l0 - E_l0) / np.sqrt(Var_star)\n\ndef range(n_boot):\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\n        boot = clean_arr[idx]\n        d = (contam_val - np.mean(boot)) / np.std(boot, ddof=1)\n        ds.append(d)\n    ds = np.array(ds)\n
